Result: 在两个数据集上，DMoA相比GPT-4o基线在最可能诊断准确率上提升10.21个百分点，安全率提升11.36个百分点。消融实验表明增益不仅源于更多模型或更长输出，也反映了结构化工作流的贡献。DMoA在4*2结构、更强的基础模型和更大的token预算下表现更优。

Conclusion: 研究成果证明了DMoA在临床任务中的潜力，并建议进一步探索多智能体框架在医学诊断中的应用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show potential for medical tasks, but their single-turn question-answer format does not reflect how clinical diagnosis is performed in practice. As a result, they remain limited in complex diagnostic settings. We developed Debate-Mixture-of-Agents (DMoA), a novel multi-agent framework that structures role-based interaction to support iterative diagnostic reasoning. Base models and DMoA were evaluated on 297 rare disease cases and 1,719 challenging cases. Across both datasets, DMoA improved most likely diagnosis accuracy by 10.21 percentage points and safety rate by 11.36 percentage points over GPT-4o baseline. Ablation experiments showed that the gains were not simply due to the use of more models or longer outputs, but also reflected the contribution of the structured workflow. Further analyses examined how framework design, base model choice, and token budget affected performance. DMoA performed better with a 4*2 structure, stronger base models, and a larger token budget. These findings demonstrate the potential of DMoA for clinical tasks and suggest further investigation of multi-agent frameworks.

</details>


### [134] [Influence Score and Transformers interpretability: Measure of the Effective Impact of Attention Heads at inference time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05074)
*Lisa Bouger,Yannick Teglia,Philippe Loubet Moundi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种影响力分数，用于量化Transformer模型中注意力头对提示注入检测分类决策的贡献。该分数结合了对logits的方向性影响和残差流内的结构性贡献，支持头、层、网络多尺度分析。在专用的DeBERTa模型上验证，揭示了正确与错误预测之间的不同决策行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在细粒度电路分析与全局基于输出的方法之间存在权衡，缺乏一种既能细致刻画注意力头作用、又能系统理解Transformer分类器决策机制的统一框架。

Method: 提出一种影响力分数，将注意力头对logits的方向性影响与其在残差流中的结构性贡献相结合，实现头级、层级和网络级的多尺度归因分析。

Result: 在专门用于提示注入检测的DeBERTa模型上的应用表明，正确预测与错误预测展现出不同的决策行为模式，验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法在细粒度电路分析与全局输出方法之间提供了有效的折中方案，为系统研究Transformer分类器的决策机制提供了一条新途径。

Abstract: We propose an influence score to quantify the contribution of attention heads to classification decisions in Transformer-based models designed for prompt injection detection. The score combines directional influence on the logits with structural contribution within the residual stream, enabling a multi-scale analysis at the head, layer, and network levels. Applied to a DeBERTa model specialized for prompt injection detection, our framework reveals distinct decision behaviours between correct and erroneous predictions. Our method provides an effective compromise between fine-grained circuit analysis and global output-based methods, and offers a systematic way to study decision mechanisms in Transformer classifiers.

</details>


### [135] [Improving Language Identification for Code-Switched Utterances with Integer Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05099)
*Joanna Radoła,Josep Maria Crego,François Yvon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了MaskLID（一种无需训练、可检测任意语言组合的代码切换识别方法），揭示了其过度依赖词级语言关联分数这一主要缺陷，将底层优化算法重构为整数线性规划（ILP），并通过清晰可解释的约束条件在10种语言的实验中显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码切换（CS）话语的自动识别对语言识别系统仍具挑战性，导致此类文本在大语言模型训练数据中代表性不足。MaskLID作为目前最优的CS识别方法虽无需训练，但存在过度依赖词级语言关联分数的问题，限制了其识别性能。

Method: 作者提出了三项主要贡献：（a）揭示并解决MaskLID过度依赖词级语言关联分数这一重要问题；（b）将底层优化算法重新表述为整数线性规划（ILP），从而能够试验大量清晰且可解释的约束条件；（c）在10种不同语言的实验中，通过上述改进大幅提升基线系统的性能。

Result: 在涉及10种多样语言的实验中取得了显著的性能提升，在CS基准测试上观察到强劲的效果改善。代码和数据已公开发布以供复现。

Conclusion: 通过将MaskLID重新表述为整数线性规划并加入解释性约束，有效解决了其词级关联分数过度依赖问题，在多个语言的代码切换识别基准上大幅超越原有系统，为无需训练的CS识别提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Automatic identification of code-switched (CS) utterances remains a challenge for language identification (LID) systems, causing such texts to be underrepresented in the training data of Large Language Models. In this paper, we revisit MaskLID, a state-of-the art approach for CS identification, which requires no training and detects arbitrary language combinations. We make three main contributions: (a) we reveal, and address, a major issue of MaskLID: its overreliance on word-level language association scores; (b) we reformulate the underlying optimization algorithm as an Integer Linear Program, enabling us to experiment with a large set of clear and interpretable constraints; (c) each of these improvements vastly improves the baseline system, as we illustrate in experiments involving 10~diverse languages, where we observe a strong boost in performance on CS benchmarks. We release our code and data for reproducibility.

</details>


### [136] [NS-ST-GraphRAG: Neuro-Symbolic Spatio-Temporal GraphRAG for Literary Knowledge Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05139)
*Zheng Kui Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了NS-ST-GraphRAG，一种面向长篇文学叙事的神经符号时空图检索增强生成框架，结合本体引导抽取、确定性约束检查、双时间坐标和空间场景属性进行动态子图检索，并引入首个面向中国古典文学的多跳问答基准Red-Chamber-QA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇文学叙事对检索增强生成构成独特的信息处理挑战：相关证据分散于各章节、实体关系随叙事时间演变、正确答案可能同时依赖时间、空间和关系三重约束。现有方法从单一语料级图中检索，无法处理图状态随时间动态变化的问题。

Method: 提出NS-ST-GraphRAG框架，包含四个核心组件：(1)本体引导的事件与关系抽取；(2)确定性时空约束检查；(3)双时间坐标（叙事时间与现实时间）与空间场景属性的建模；(4)动态子图检索——根据查询的时间与空间范围选择有效的图状态进行检索并在可追溯证据上生成答案。同时构建基准数据集Red-Chamber-QA。

Result: 在120道预留测试集上，NS-ST-GraphRAG的机械答案复现率0.733，优于冻结窗口基线0.675和闭卷模型0.083（McNemar精确p=0.092，方向有利但未达显著）；语义裁判准确率为0.866对比0.850。预指定的约束类别假设H2未被交付实验比较所支持。

Conclusion: 结果表明时间图表示、约束抽取与可审计评估可以整合为统一框架，实现长篇叙事的可验证知识处理，并为该类问题提供了新的基准和评测范式。

Abstract: Long-form literary narratives pose a distinctive information-processing challenge for retrieval-augmented generation: relevant evidence is distributed across chapters, relations evolve over narrative time, and correct answers may depend jointly on temporal, spatial, and relational constraints. We propose NS-ST-GraphRAG, a neuro-symbolic spatio-temporal GraphRAG framework that integrates ontology-guided extraction, deterministic constraint checking, dual temporal coordinates, spatial scene attributes, and dynamic sub-graph retrieval. Instead of retrieving from a single corpus-level graph, the framework selects the graph state valid for the temporal and spatial scope of a query and grounds generated answers in traceable evidence. We further introduce Red-Chamber-QA, to our knowledge the first open multi-hop question-answering benchmark for classical Chinese literature, with time-, space-, and general-question categories, per-part evidence spans, and deterministic shortcut controls. On a 120-question held-out split, NS-ST-GraphRAG achieves mechanical answer reproduction of 0.733 versus 0.675 for the frozen window baseline and 0.083 for a closed-book model (McNemar exact p = 0.092, directionally favorable but not significant); semantic-judge accuracy is 0.866 versus 0.850. The pre-specified constrained-category condition of H2 is not supported by the delivered comparison. These results show how temporal graph representation, constrained extraction, and auditable evaluation integrate into a unified framework for verifiable knowledge processing over long-form narrative.

</details>


### [137] [A Human-in-the-Loop Framework for AI-Assisted Scoring in Large-Scale Writing Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05143)
*María Eugenia Curi,Germán Capdehourat,Isabel Amigo,Magdalena Romano,Rosana Serra,Adrián Silveira,Andrés Peri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在大型全国性评估中用于短篇书面回答（约150-200字）的AI辅助评分框架，采用人在回路（human-in-the-loop）策略。基于两届全国性考试各约5000份真实学生答卷数据，研究分析了AI评分与人工评分在多个评分维度上的一致性，以及所提出的决策流程对通过/不通过结果的影响。结果显示多数维度下模型与人工评估达到中到高度一致，校正工作流能有效识别最需要人工复核的情况，表明AI辅助评分需与精心设计的人工监督相结合方可安全整合进大规模评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的大规模教育评估完全依赖人工评分，成本高昂且效率有限。随着AI尤其是大语言模型的发展，研究者希望探索其能否在不牺牲评分质量的前提下提升评分流程的效率和可扩展性，从而在真实运营环境中验证AI辅助评分的可行性与安全性。

Method: 研究在真实运营背景下进行，采用两届全国性测试数据（各约5000份学生作答），针对约150-200字的短篇书面文本，构建了一个AI辅助评分框架，核心包含：（1）AI自动评分模块；（2）人在回路（human-in-the-loop）的校正决策流程，用于识别需要人工复核的高价值案例。研究分析了AI生成分数与多个评分维度下人工评分者之间的一致性，并评估决策流程对最终通过/不通过结果的影响。

Result: 研究结果显示：在大多数评分维度上，AI模型与人工评估之间呈现中度到高度的一致性，支持了AI辅助在该情境下的可行性。所提出的校正工作流能够有效识别人工复核最具有价值的案例，从而更高效地分配专家评审精力。研究发现AI辅助评分只有在配备精心设计的人工监督机制时才能安全地整合进大规模评估流程。

Conclusion: 论文认为AI辅助评分可以在全国性评估系统中安全部署，前提是结合审慎设计的人工监督机制。文章讨论了在实际国家评估系统中的部署启示，并提出了未来研究方向，包括对模型与人工评分对齐情况的纵向监测，以及分析AI支持的评审工作流可能引入的认知偏差。

Abstract: The integration of artificial intelligence (AI), particularly large language models (LLMs), into educational assessment has opened new opportunities to enhance the efficiency and scalability of grading processes. This study presents the design and validation of an AI-assisted scoring framework for written responses in a large-scale national assessment. The proposed approach focuses on short written texts of approximately 150-200 words and incorporates a human-in-the-loop strategy to preserve assessment quality while reducing manual workload. The study is grounded in a real operational context, using data from two recent editions of a nationwide test, each comprising approximately 5,000 student responses. We analyze the alignment between AI-generated scores and human raters across multiple rubric dimensions, as well as the impact of the proposed decision flow on pass/fail outcomes. Results show moderate to high agreement between the model and human evaluations in most dimensions, supporting the feasibility of AI assistance in this setting. Moreover, the proposed correction workflow identifies cases where human review is most valuable, enabling a more efficient allocation of expert effort. The findings suggest that AI-assisted scoring can be safely integrated into large-scale assessment processes only when combined with carefully designed human oversight. The paper concludes by discussing practical implications for deployment in national assessment systems and outlining future research directions, including longitudinal monitoring of model-human alignment and the analysis of potential cognitive bias introduced by AI-supported review workflows.

</details>


### [138] [From Vision to Language: Investigating Causal Information Flow in Multimodal Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05149)
*Davide Testa,Hugh Mee Wong,Alessandro Lenci,Bernardo Magnini,Albert Gatt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过层间因果干预方法追踪视频-文本注意力路径中的跨模态信息流，发现视觉信息主要在模型处理候选答案选项时被整合，名词作为语义锚点在多模态增强中起关键作用，动词在时间关系处理中更为重要，并揭示了VLMs在时间推理上的脆弱性可能部分源于特定时间表达相关的语言偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Vision-Language Models评估通常只看最终预测结果，但无法判断这些决策是否真正基于视觉证据。作者旨在从机制层面理解视觉信息如何影响基于语言的决策，进而揭示模型推理的grounding程度。

Method: 在视频生成式多项选择任务中，对视频-文本注意力通路应用逐层(layer-wise)因果干预方法，分别针对空间、因果和时间三种视觉推理类型，追踪跨模态信息流。

Result: (1) 视觉信息主要在模型处理候选答案选项时被整合，答案选项是最终决策的主要文本grounding位点；(2) 名词作为多模态语义富集阶段的重要语义锚点，动词则在处理时间关系时更具相关性；(3) 时间推理呈现独特模式，VLMs难以重构跨视频帧的序列信息，但该脆弱性可能同时反映特定时间表达词语所引入的语言偏差。

Conclusion: VLMs的视觉证据利用存在不均匀分布——决策主要锚定于答案选项而非题干，词性(e.g.,名词vs.动词)在不同推理类型中发挥差异化作用，且时间推理的困难需同时归因于模型自身的序列信息重构能力和训练数据中的语言先验偏差。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models are commonly evaluated through their final predictions, but understanding whether these decisions are grounded in visual evidence requires tracing how visual information contributes to language-based decisions. With this purpose in mind, we investigate cross-modal information flow in a video-based generative multiple-choice-like setting by applying a layer-wise causal intervention on video-text attention pathways. We target spatial, causal, and temporal visual reasoning. Our results show that visual information is mainly integrated while the model processes the candidate answer options, which serve as the primary textual grounding sites for the final decision. We further show that nouns play an important role as semantic anchors during multimodal enrichment, while verbs are more relevant when temporal relations are processed. Finally, we identify a distinct pattern in temporal reasoning, suggesting that VLMs struggle to reconstruct sequential information across video frames, but we remark that such fragility may also reflect linguistic biases associated with specific temporal expressions used for defining the relation between events within a scene.

</details>


### [139] [Compression Beyond the Uncompressed: A Two-Stage Training Recipe for Soft Context Compression in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05152)
*Shuyu Guo,Shuo Zhang,Zhaochun Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DEX-Comp两阶段训练方法用于RAG软上下文压缩：纯蒸馏阶段在无压缩RAG正确响应上热启动压缩模型，硬探索阶段仅对无压缩RAG失败的查询进行强化学习，从而突破传统蒸馏方法的性能瓶颈。在五个开放域QA基准上实现16倍压缩和4-24倍推理加速，性能与无压缩RAG基线相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过引入外部知识增强语言模型，但冗长的检索上下文导致输入膨胀和推理效率下降。软上下文压缩虽能缩短嵌入序列，但现有方法依赖从无压缩RAG系统中蒸馏输出进行训练，性能天然受限于原始模型，无法超越其表达能力。

Method: 提出DEX-Comp两阶段训练方案：第一阶段为纯蒸馏（Pure Distillation），仅在无压缩RAG的正确答案上对压缩模型进行热启动；第二阶段为硬探索（Hard Exploration），仅针对无压缩RAG失败的查询运行强化学习，迫使模型探索更适合压缩表示的计算模式，从而突破蒸馏上限。

Result: 在五个开放域QA基准上、top-5到top-30的检索深度下，DEX-Comp将检索上下文压缩16倍，推理速度提升4-24倍，性能与无压缩RAG基线相当或超越。消融实验和跨数据集、跨骨干网络的评估验证了每个阶段的贡献和方法泛化性。

Conclusion: DEX-Comp通过两阶段训练策略有效解决了现有软上下文压缩方法受限于蒸馏上限的问题，兼顾压缩效率与回答质量，在多种检索深度和数据集上均表现出鲁棒性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances language models with external knowledge, but the lengthy retrieved context inflates the input and degrades inference efficiency. Soft context compression encodes each document into a substantially shorter embedding sequence. However, most existing approaches are trained by distilling outputs from uncompressed RAG systems, inherently limiting their performance relative to the original model. To address this limitation, we propose DEX-Comp, a two-stage training recipe: Pure Distillation warm-starts the compression model on the uncompressed RAG's correct responses only, and Hard Exploration then runs reinforcement learning solely on queries the uncompressed RAG fails, forcing the model to explore computation patterns better suited to compressed representations. On five open-domain QA benchmarks at retrieval depths from top-5 to top-30, DEX-Comp compresses retrieved contexts by $16\times$ and accelerates inference by $4\times$--$24\times$, while achieving performance comparable to or exceeding the uncompressed RAG baseline across retrieval depths. Ablations and evaluations across diverse datasets and backbones further confirm the contribution of each stage and the generalization of our approach.

</details>


### [140] [Can Large Language Models Anticipate Behavioral Responses to Social Policies? A Case of Pension Enrollment Prediction among China's Flexible Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05189)
*Yumiao Li,Peixin Liu,Donglin Di,Chen Li,Runhuan Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FlexPension-LLM，首个面向中国灵活就业人员养老金参保预测任务的领域专精大语言模型，并引入DKI-RDistill方法将政策依据线索注入提示词，通过LoRA/SFT将推理增强的监督信号蒸馏到开源MoE学生模型中。在CHFS 2019盲测分割上达到0.9316的综合F1分数，超越其教师模型及15/17个基线，与Claude Opus 4.6统计上无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估社会政策变化的影响对政策制定者而言是一大挑战：计量经济学方法在推断到假设性情境时可能不可靠，而实地试点项目成本高昂。因此需要一种更可靠的、可扩展的政策评估工具，本文探索将通用大语言模型适配为政策评估工具的可能性。

Method: 提出FlexPension-LLM模型与DKI-RDistill方法。DKI-RDistill将政策依据线索注入提示词，包括基于Probit模型的边际效应以及户籍-省份养老金规则；随后使用LoRA/SFT将推理增强的监督信号蒸馏进开源权重MoE学生模型中，并对教师模型在真实标签下出错的案例进行重新生成修正。

Result: 在CHFS 2019盲测分割上，FlexPension-LLM达到0.9316综合F1，超越其Claude Sonnet 4.5教师模型和15/17个基线，并与Claude Opus 4.6统计上无显著差异。在四个外部调查中，平均综合F1为0.7549，且在最强系统中表现范围最窄。成分分析表明增益主要来自政策依据线索注入与错误过滤监督，推理轨迹可用于对照政策规则核查。

Conclusion: 大语言模型经领域专业化改造后可作为可靠的政策评估工具。政策依据线索注入和错误过滤的监督蒸馏是关键增益来源，且模型输出的推理轨迹提供了可核查的决策依据，增强了政策评估的可解释性与可信度。

Abstract: Assessing the impacts of social policy changes is a widely acknowledged challenge for policymakers. Econometric methods can be unreliable when extrapolating to hypothetical scenarios, while field pilot programs are highly costly. In this paper, we propose using large language models (LLMs) as policy-assessment tools adapted from general-purpose models. We present FlexPension-LLM, the first domain-specialized large language model for a hierarchical pension-enrollment prediction task among flexible workers in China, and introduce DKI-RDistill, which injects policy-grounded cues into the prompt, including Probit-derived marginal effects and hukou-province pension rules. The method then uses LoRA/SFT to distill rationale-augmented supervision into an open-weight MoE student, with teacher errors corrected by regenerating those cases under ground-truth labels. On a CHFS 2019 blind split, FlexPension-LLM achieves 0.9316 Composite F1, surpassing its Claude Sonnet 4.5 teacher and 15 of 17 baselines, and is statistically indistinguishable from Claude Opus 4.6. Across four external surveys, it averages 0.7549 Composite F1 and shows the narrowest performance range among the strongest systems. Component analysis shows that gains come mainly from policy-grounded cue injection and error-filtered supervision, while rationales provide decision traces that can be checked against policy rules.

</details>


### [141] [A Verifier-Guided Explainable Reasoning Framework with Gold-Anchored QLoRA, Task-Aware Mixture-of-Experts, and Group-Relative RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05221)
*Thi Kim Trang Vo,Nam Tien Le,Thi Kim Nguyet Vo,Minh Khang Tran,Duy Phuong Tran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种验证器引导的可解释推理框架，用于透明教育问答，结合金锚定QLoRA、任务感知符号路由和组相对RLVR，在438个保留样本上使P3推理深度从50.68%提升至72.20%，P1混合准确率稳定在55.94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽具较强推理能力，但其解释常存在不一致、缺乏严谨依据或难以验证的问题，尤其在教育问答这一需要透明、可验证推理的领域，现有方法难以满足要求。

Method: 提出验证器引导的可解释推理框架：1) 使用金锚定(权威答案)的字段加权QLoRA微调Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct；2) 轻量路由将逻辑题分配给FOL/Z3验证器、物理题分配给公式及单位感知符号求解器；3) 在RLVR阶段利用验证器反馈进行候选评估、自我修订和奖励构建；4) 按P1(答案正确性)、P2(证据/单位一致性)、P3(推理深度与可解释性)三个维度评估候选响应；5) 推理阶段使用无金标准自一致性聚合多个响应，物理题可选问题仅问答式验证器做保守系统级修正。

Result: 在438个保留样本上：RLVR将P3从50.68%提升至72.20%；混合P1保持约55.94%稳定；自一致性将纯模型P1从48.86%提升至50.23%，其余混合增益来自符号验证器。

Conclusion: RLVR主要增强显式推理结构（P3显著提升），而符号验证在系统层面补充神经策略、提升答案可靠性（P1增益主要由符号验证提供）。两者互补，共同实现透明教育问答中的可验证推理。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong reasoning ability, but their explanations can remain inconsistent, weakly grounded, or difficult to verify. We propose a verifier-guided explainable reasoning framework for transparent educational question answering that combines gold-anchored QLoRA, task-aware symbolic routing, and group-relative RLVR. Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct is first adapted with field-weighted QLoRA supervision anchored to authoritative answers. A lightweight router then assigns logic problems to a FOL/Z3 verifier and physics problems to a formula- and unit aware symbolic solver. Verifier feedback is further used to support candidate evaluation, self-revision, and reward construction during RLVR. Candidate responses are evaluated along three complementary dimensions: P1 for answer correctness, P2 for evidence or unit consistency, and P3 for reasoning depth and explainability. At inference, gold-free self-consistency aggregates multiple candidate responses before an optional question-only physics verifier performs conservative system-level correction. On 438 held-out examples, RLVR increases P3 from 50.68% to 72.20%, while hybrid P1 remains approximately stable at 55.94%. Self-consistency improves model only P1 from 48.86% to 50.23%, with symbolic verification providing the remaining hybrid gain. These results indicate that RLVR primarily strengthens explicit reasoning structure, while symbolic verification complements the neural policy by improving answer reliability at the system level.

</details>


### [142] [Self-Supervised Lexical Representation Learning for Fast, Large-Scale Phylogenetic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05262)
*Tim Wientzek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个完全自监督的对比学习框架，直接从IPA词表中学习词汇表示，无需同源标注、比对或专家输入，进而推断涵盖3,399种语言变体的全球系统发育树。推断树与Glottolog参考树的广义四元距离与多个基线相当，且仅需在普通笔记本GPU上运行数分钟；同一表示还能捕捉历时概念稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算系统发育学已成为历史语言学的重要工具，但在全球尺度应用受两大制约：一是基于字符方法所需的同源判定依赖大量人工标注，二是大数据集上的推断计算成本高昂。现有方法难以在无标注情况下高效完成大规模系统发育推断。

Method: 提出完全自监督的对比学习框架，直接学习原始IPA转写词表的词汇表示。采用双重对比目标：(1)词级损失将语音相似的形式组织为连贯空间；(2)语言级辅助损失促使词汇空间反映语言更广泛的音系属性。由词表示推导成对语言距离，用于推断系统发育树。同时用跨语言成对距离的方差计算概念稳定性排名。

Result: 推断的3,399种语言变体系统发育树与Glottolog参考树的广义四元距离（GQD）与多个基线相当，且只需普通笔记本GPU上数分钟计算。概念稳定性排名与既有排名显著相关。消融实验表明语言级目标和语音特征向量的使用均改善推断树拓扑（以GQD衡量）。

Conclusion: 该框架为大规模系统发育推断提供了计算高效且全自动的替代方案，并提供支持语言级与概念级下游分析的统一表示。

Abstract: Computational phylogenetics has become an essential tool in historical linguistics, yet its application at a global scale remains constrained by two factors: the labor-intensive manual annotation of cognacy judgments required for character-based methods and the substantial computational cost of inference on large datasets. This paper introduces a fully self-supervised contrastive learning framework that learns lexical representations directly from raw IPA-transcribed wordlists, without requiring cognacy annotations, alignments, or additional expert input. The model employs a dual contrastive objective: a word-level loss that organizes phonetically similar forms into a coherent space, and an auxiliary language-level loss that encourages the lexical space to reflect broader phonological properties of languages. From the resulting word representations, pairwise language distances are derived and used to infer a global phylogenetic tree of 3,399 language varieties. The inferred tree achieves a generalized quartet distance (GQD) to the Glottolog reference tree competitive with multiple baselines, while requiring only minutes of computation on a standard notebook GPU. Furthermore, the same representations capture diachronic concept stability: variance in pairwise distances across languages yields stability rankings that correlate significantly with established rankings. Ablation studies confirm that both the language-level objective and the use of phonetic feature vectors improved the inferred trees topology with regards to GQD. The framework thus provides a computationally efficient and fully automatic alternative for large-scale phylogenetic inference and offers a unified representation supporting downstream analyses at both the language and concept level.

</details>


### [143] [LexFlip: A Dissociation Diagnostic for Legal Meaning Preservation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05296)
*Gaurab Baral*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出当前用于验证简化法律条款语义保持性的评测方法存在缺陷——因为「同义对得分最高、无关对得分最低」的检查会把词汇重叠与法律效力耦合在一起，任何关于词元重叠的单调函数都能满足。作者提出「解耦」（dissociation）方案，即保持表层形式不变而移动法律效力。作者发布 LexFlip 数据集（373个魁北克法语成文法的最小扰动，反转法律效力同时保留0.93的词元），用于评测各类打分模型。结果显示七种嵌入与BERTScore指标在该编辑上仅使用了其「相同-无关」量程的0.022-0.039，而双向NLI达到0.670；在FrJudge上，一个仅用长度的特征优于所有语义指标，且与人工上限（r=0.597）的差距最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一致性检查（同义对分数最高、无关对分数最低）无法真正证明简化条款与原文语义等价，因为该方法将词汇重叠与法律效力混为一谈——任何词元重叠的单调函数都能通过这些检查。作者希望设计一种能真正隔离「表层形式」与「法律效力」的评测方法，以揭示语义相似度指标在法律文本简化验证中的失真程度。

Method: 提出「解耦」概念：构造一个评测条目，其表层形式（token）保持不变而法律效力发生反转。据此构建LexFlip数据集——373个魁北克法定法语文本的最小扰动，反转法律效力同时保留约93%的词元。利用该数据基准对标量指标、回归器与受提示的评判模型进行统一评测，涵盖七种嵌入及BERTScore指标、双向NLI方法，并引入包含长度特征的FrJudge基准对比人工上限。

Result: 七种嵌入与BERTScore指标在LexFlip的语义反转编辑上，仅使用其「相同对—无关对」评分量程的0.022到0.039；双向NLI族则达到0.670（该家族恰是被「同义对检查」会淘汰的）。在FrJudge上，以人工上限r=0.597为参照，单纯的长度特征超越了所有语义度量，且拥有测得的与人工上限之间最小的边际差距。

Conclusion: 现有语义相似度度量在法律文本上严重低估表层形式微小变化所能带来的法律效力的实质反转，说明现行一致性检查与度量在法律简化领域不可靠。所提出的解耦评估思路与LexFlip数据可作为更严谨的基准来筛选真正捕捉法律语义变化的指标，而长度这类琐碎特征竟能在实践中胜过语义模型，凸显出现有方法需要重新设计的急迫性。

Abstract: Does a simplified legal clause still say what the original said? The checks in current use cannot establish that it does: requiring an identical pair to score highest and an unrelated pair lowest moves lexical overlap and legal force together, so any monotone function of token overlap satisfies both. Our remedy is a dissociation, an item holding surface form fixed while legal force moves. We release LexFlip, 373 minimal perturbations of Quebec statutory French that reverse legal force while preserving 0.93 of the tokens, with a harness scoring metrics, regressors and prompted judges alike. The seven embedding and BERTScore metrics we test spend only 0.022 to 0.039 of their identical-to-unrelated range on such an edit, against 0.670 for bidirectional NLI, the one family the identical-pair check would disqualify. On FrJudge, against a measured human ceiling of r=0.597, a bare length feature outscores every semantic metric and has the lowest margin we measure.

</details>


### [144] [WearableQA: A Benchmark for Health Reasoning over Real-World Wearable Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05405)
*Ji Soo Lee,Xilun Chen,Pierce Chuang,Ashish Shenoy,Jason Wei,Dohwan Ko,Hyunwoo J. Kim,Benoit Corda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WearableQA是一个基于200名真实用户、最多500天可穿戴时序数据（含血液生物标志物和人口统计学信息）构建的基准测试，包含4,084道10选1选择题。该基准通过双轴16种题型设计（数据vs健康推理、单信号vs跨信号推理），评估LLM对真实纵向可穿戴数据的推理能力。评估14种LLM显示性能从19.6%到72.9%，多数模型低于60%，说明该基准远未被解决，能有效区分模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴传感技术可连续监测生理和行为信号，但缺乏评估AI系统能否对真实用户长期可穿戴记录进行推理的基准测试。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建一个保留真实数据分布（含设备噪声和个体差异）的诊断性基准。

Method: (1) 从200名真实用户构建数据集，每个用户最多500天日志，涵盖可穿戴时间序列、血液生物标志物和人口统计数据；生成4,084道10选项多项选择题。(2) 引入16种问题类型，沿两个互补维度组织：数据vs健康推理（区分纵向测量的计算与生理学解释），单个vs跨信号推理（区分单信号推理与多信号整合）。(3) 采用双重基础框架：结合文献基础的生理发现与统计验证的人群基础生理模式，确保问题可靠且能捕捉真实可穿戴数据中的有意义关系。

Result: 评估14种专有和开源LLM，WearableQA有效区分模型能力，性能范围从19.6%到72.9%（随机基线为10%）。该基准远未被解决，大多数模型准确率低于60%。

Conclusion: WearableQA为评估LLM对真实世界可穿戴数据的推理提供了现实且具诊断性的基准。结果表明现有LLM在该任务上仍有显著提升空间，尤其在健康推理和跨信号整合等复杂推理维度上。

Abstract: Recent advances in wearable sensing enable continuous monitoring of physiological and behavioral signals, yet existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether AI systems can reason over a real user's longitudinal wearable record. We introduce WearableQA, a benchmark comprising 4,084 10-option multiple-choice questions constructed from the wearable time series, blood biomarkers, and demographics of 200 real users, each with up to 500 days of daily measurements. WearableQA preserves authentic wearable distributions that include device noise and inter-individual variability. To evaluate distinct reasoning capabilities, we introduce 16 question types organized along two complementary axes: data versus health reasoning, which distinguishes computation over longitudinal measurements from physiological interpretation; and single- versus cross-signal reasoning, which separates reasoning about individual signals from the integration of multiple signals. To construct reliable questions at scale, we adopt a dual-grounding framework that combines literature-grounded physiological findings with statistically validated population-grounded physiological patterns. This enables the capture of meaningful relationships observed in real-world wearable data. Evaluation of 14 proprietary and open-source LLMs demonstrates that WearableQA effectively differentiates model capabilities, with performance ranging from 19.6% to 72.9% against a 10% chance baseline. Moreover, WearableQA remains far from solved: most models achieve accuracies below 60%. Overall, WearableQA provides a realistic and diagnostic benchmark for evaluating LLM reasoning over real-world wearable data.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [EXAONE Forecast for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04239)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本技术报告提出EXAONE Finance，一个针对金融时间序列预测的专用基础模型。它采用无注意力架构（用因果一维卷积和分组感知池化MLP替代自注意力），结合掩码上下文增强提升对缺失数据的鲁棒性，在涵盖股票、外汇等多元资产的金融语料上预训练后，在FinVerse基准上三个评估层级均达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型（TSFM）主要面向通用领域，存在三重局限：1）基于自注意力的计算成本随序列长度和变量数量二次增长，难以应对金融中长序列多通道数据；2）假设输入完全观测，但金融市场普遍存在数据缺失；3）预训练语料无法捕捉金融市场的独特动态。这些不足阻碍了通用TSFM在金融领域的有效应用。

Method: EXAONE Finance采用注意力自由架构，用两个线性时间复杂度的算子替代自注意力：1）因果一维卷积负责时间维度混合；2）分组感知池化MLP负责变量维度混合。同时引入掩码上下文增强技术，在训练中暴露连续的缺失片段，提升对金融数据中普遍缺失情况的鲁棒性。模型在大规模金融语料上预训练，涵盖股票、外汇、商品、加密资产、固定收益和宏观经济指标等多类资产。

Result: 在FinVerse这一覆盖多元资产类别的金融预测基准上，EXAONE Finance在全部三个评估层级——点预测准确率、横截面资产排序和投资组合盈利能力上均排名第一，达到当前最优水平。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，针对金融特定领域设计无注意力架构并配合缺失鲁棒训练与金融专属语料预训练，能够在金融时间序列预测任务上显著超越通用TSFM，验证了领域专用时间序列基础模型的有效性。

Abstract: This technical report presents EXAONE Forecast for Finance (EXAONE Finance), a financial time series (TS) foundation model (TSFM) tailored to financial forecasting. Recent TSFMs achieve strong zero-shot performance through large-scale pretraining. However, they are primarily developed for general-domain TS and largely rely on self-attention backbones whose computational cost grows quadratically with sequence length and variate count. Moreover, they assume fully observed inputs and are pretrained on corpora that fail to capture the unique dynamics of financial markets. These limitations hinder their applicability to finance, where long, many-channel, intermittently observed panels are common. To address these challenges, EXAONE Finance adopts an attention-free architecture, replacing self-attention with two simple yet effective linear-time operators: 1) a causal 1D convolution for temporal mixing and 2) a group-aware pooling multi-layer perceptron (MLP) for variate mixing. Furthermore, a masked context augmentation exposes the model to contiguous missing spans during training, improving robustness to the missingness pervasive in financial markets. EXAONE Finance is pretrained on a large-scale financial corpus covering not only equities but also foreign exchange, commodities, crypto-assets, fixed income, and macroeconomic indicators. On FinVerse, a financial forecasting benchmark covering diverse asset classes, EXAONE Finance attains state-of-the-art performance, ranking first across all three evaluation tiers---point-forecast accuracy, cross-sectional asset ranking, and portfolio profitability.

</details>


### [146] [From Matching Models to Recruiting Agents: A Systematized Narrative Review of AI Recruitment Systems, Evaluation, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04286)
*Ziyi Zhao,Guanzheng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过系统化叙述性综述，梳理了人工智能招聘从双向检索与行为排序，经神经人岗匹配、大语言模型组件，到工具型招聘代理的演进历程。作者对40篇代表性工作进行分析，提出三个耦合转变（相似性到互适性、单一模型到复合工作流、离线预测到证据与生产力对齐的评估），并指出当前评估在隐私、公平性、效用和安全维度上存在系统性缺口，最终提出从评估证据到可辩护主张的分级映射及研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 招聘自动化对象已从简单的档案配对与排序列表，演变为检索证据、比较候选人并支持或执行行动的多阶段工作流。既有综述多为流行率估算，缺乏对该演进过程及多重耦合转变的系统性剖析，也未厘清不同层级证据（字段级、配对级、列表级、案例级、轨迹级、结果级）与可推导主张之间的对应关系，因此亟需一套综合性的系统化综述与分析框架。

Method: 采用系统化叙述性综述方法，运用目的性检索与编码协议（更新至2026年7月23日，并补充至2026年9月2日），从40篇代表性工作及配套的工业与法律来源中提取证据。分析聚焦于跨越文档理解、检索、排序、评估、面试、寻源与人机交接等环节所呈现的三大耦合转变，并在字段、配对、列表、案例、轨迹与结果六个层级上区分证据类型。

Result: 揭示出若干长期缺口：行为标签混淆了曝光、偏好与资质信号；私有与合成数据限制外部效度；最终输出分数掩盖管道级故障；在所编码的样本中隐私未被直接评估，且没有一行工作同时评估效用、公平性、隐私与安全等维度。

Conclusion: 引入从评估证据到最强可辩护主张的分级映射机制，并提出面向互适性、证据支撑、时间控制、选择性及可审计系统的研究议程。判断进展的标准应为：工作流是否检索到正确证据、保留不确定性、支持可异议的决策，并在明确成本与风险约束下改善结果。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence in recruitment has shifted the object being automated from profile pairs and ranked lists to multi-stage workflows that retrieve evidence, compare candidates, and support or execute actions. This systematized narrative review traces that development from bilateral retrieval and behavioral ranking through neural person--job matching, large language model (LLM) components, and tool-using recruiting agents. Using a purposive search and coding protocol updated through 23 July 2026, plus targeted updates through 2 September 2026, we organize 40 representative works with supporting industrial and legal sources. This synthesis is not a prevalence estimate. We analyze three coupled transitions: from similarity to reciprocal suitability, from a model to a compound workflow, and from offline prediction to evidence- and productivity-aligned evaluation. Across document understanding, retrieval, ranking, assessment, interviewing, sourcing, and human handoff, we distinguish field-, pair-, list-, case-, trajectory-, and outcome-level evidence. Persistent gaps arise because behavioral labels confound exposure, preference, and qualification; private and synthetic data limit external validity; final-output scores conceal pipeline failures; and, within the coded set, privacy is not directly evaluated and no row jointly evaluates utility, fairness, privacy, and security. These observations describe the coded set rather than the field as a whole. We therefore introduce a staged mapping from evaluation evidence to the strongest defensible claim, together with an agenda for reciprocal, evidence-grounded, temporally controlled, selective, and auditable systems. Progress should be judged by whether workflows retrieve the right evidence, preserve uncertainty, support contestable decisions, and improve outcomes under explicit cost and risk constraints.

</details>


### [147] [Harbor Adapters and Harbor-Index: Infrastructure and a Curated Meta-Dataset for Large-Scale Agentic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04298)
*Lin Shi,Haowei Lin,Zixuan Zhu,Xiaoyue Zhou,Xiang Li,Xiangning Lin,Yaxuan Deng,Han Xu,Yuangang Li,Shanda Li,Zizhao Chen,Hanwen Xing,Harsh Raj,Bo Chen,Quan Shi,Steven Dillmann,Yipeng Gao,Puneesh Khanna,Ruofan Lu,Chao Beyond Zhou,Michael Yang,Robert Zhang,Siyuan Chai,Jiayu Chang,Yizhao Chen,Xiaokun Chen,Yiwei Dai,Wenting Yang,Hange Liu,Minghao Liu,Zihan Wang,Adnan El Assadi,Benedikt Stroebl,E. Kelly Buchanan,Han Meng,Junwei He,Longxuan Yu,Radin Shayanfar,Yukyung Lee,Zhikang Dong,Allen G Hart,Anjiang Wei,Anurag Kashyap,Arpandeep Khatua,Audrey Jixin Zheng,Chengrui Ma,David Heineman,Dubing Chen,Hai-Anh Trinh,Haishuo Fang,Hefan Zhang,Hui Shen,Issa Sugiura,Jiankai Sun,Jiechao Gao,Junhong Lin,Junnan Li,Kai Yang,Lei Hsiung,Maoyu Wang,Mengze Tang,Nabil Omi,Negin Raoof,Nicholas Edwards,Octavia Guo,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Pengliang Ji,Przemysław Hejman,Qi Qi,Qunshu Lin,Richard Zhuang,Rui Yang,Ruichen Zheng,Ryan Marten,Shaghayegh Fazliani,Shizheng Hou,Sicong Jiang,Sijie Li,Song Bian,Terry Yue Zhuo,Tianqing Wu,Tom Tang,Wanjia Zhao,Weihao Xuan,Wenhua Liang,Xian Liu,Xin Lan,Xuan Zhang,Xuandong Zhao,Yanchuan Tang,Yifan Jiang,Yijiang Li,Yitong Guan,Yizhi Li,Yonghui Liu,Yuheng Tang,Yujun,Mao,Yunfei Zhao,Yuxin Wang,Yuxuan Tang,Zhenheng Tang,Zhifei Li,Ziruo Wang,Ziyu She,Kaiyuan Liu,Iheb Chaabane,Yuxin Tang,Xiangyi Li,Andy Konwinski,Boxuan Li,Leon Liangyu Chen,Alex Dimakis,Nicholas Carlini,Soroush Vosoughi,Di He,Etash Guha,Benjamin Feuer,Mike Merrill,Ludwig Schmidt,Alex Shaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Harbor Adapters，一个统一的智能体基准评测基础设施。它通过适配器将超过80个基准移植用于评测任意智能体，并在54个基准上大规模评测了8个模型（分别使用Terminus-2和3种原生框架之一）。同时引入Harbor-Index——一个由29个基准中精选的82个困难、多样且高质量任务组成的评测集（经难度过滤、AI和人工审计及审计修复循环）。结果显示最强配置GPT-5.5+Codex仅达28.0%通过率，且无任何模型-框架组合超过30%，表明该评测仍具挑战性。作者开源了适配器、评测结果和深入分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体基准数量不断增长，在复杂环境和智能体集成要求下对这些基准进行统一评测非常困难。作者希望构建一个可扩展的统一评测基础设施，使任意智能体能在大量基准上得到一致、可靠的评测，并提供可负担但仍有足够区分度的高质量任务集。

Method: (1) 开发benchmark adapters，将80多个基准移植为可评测任意智能体的统一接口，并通过代码审查和parity实验验证；(2) 在54个基准上对8个跨能力档次的模型进行大规模评测，每个模型都用Terminus-2及3种原生harness之一运行；(3) 通过难度过滤、AI与人工审计、以及审计-修复循环，从适配的基准套件中提炼出Harbor-Index：82个高难度、多样且高质量的任务，覆盖29个基准。

Result: 大规模评测揭示了智能体能力与失败模式的更全面图景。Harbor-Index具有足够的挑战性与区分度：无任何评测的模型-框架配置超过30%的通过率，最强的GPT-5.5+Codex达到28.0%，且评测成本可负担。

Conclusion: Harbor Adapters为智能体评测提供了统一且可扩展的基础设施，而Harbor-Index兼顾了大规模评测的挑战性与广度同时保持运行成本可控。作者以开源形式发布适配器、评测结果、深入分析及Harbor-Index，以支持更可靠、更全面的语言模型智能体评测。

Abstract: Evaluating agents on the growing number of agentic benchmarks is challenging because they often require complex environments and agent integrations. We introduce Harbor Adapters, a unified evaluation infrastructure for agentic benchmarks. Our work makes three contributions. First, we develop benchmark adapters that port more than 80 benchmarks to evaluate arbitrary agents, and validate them through rigorous code review and parity experiments. Second, we conduct a large-scale evaluation of 8 models spanning capability tiers across 54 benchmarks; every model is run with Terminus-2 and with one of 3 native harnesses. This enables a broader analysis of agent capabilities and failure modes than was previously possible. Third, we introduce Harbor-Index, a curated set of 82 difficult, diverse, and high-quality tasks spanning 29 benchmarks, refined from the adapted suite through difficulty filtering, AI and human audit, and an audit-and-fix loop. Harbor-Index preserves the challenge and breadth of large-scale agentic evaluations while being affordable to run; no evaluated model-harness configuration exceeds 30% pass rate, and the strongest (GPT-5.5 with Codex) reaches 28.0%. We release the adapters, evaluation results, in-depth analysis, and Harbor-Index as open-source artifacts to support more reliable and comprehensive evaluation of language-model agents.

</details>


### [148] [Data-Optimized Contingency Screening: A Machine Learning Approach to Power System Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04300)
*Joshua Salako,Folajimi Osikomaiya,Olakorede Olamiju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探索使用机器学习算法（KNN、随机森林、SVM）对电力系统故障事件的安全等级进行分类（安全、中等、严重）。采用牛顿-拉夫逊潮流方法结合整体性能指数（OPI）提取系统数据，使用SMOTE和PCA进行数据预处理，在IEEE-14和IEEE-30总线上通过N-k故障场景（k=1,2,3）生成数据集，并在四种混合预处理配置下评估模型性能。随机森林取得最优F1分数（IEEE-30为0.97，IEEE-14为0.86），研究表明PCA比SMOTE对整体性能贡献更大，机器学习是传统故障分析的强大可扩展替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统的安全性对稳定性和可靠性至关重要，特别是在发生中断时。有效对电力系统故障进行分类能够实现主动决策，减少大规模故障和崩溃的风险，因此需要探索更高效的故障分类方法。

Method: 使用牛顿-拉夫逊潮流方法从故障场景中提取系统数据，以整体性能指数（OPI）作为安全度量；采用SMOTE处理类别不平衡、PCA进行降维；在IEEE-14和IEEE-30节点系统上通过N-k（k=1,2,3）故障场景生成数据集，在四种混合预处理配置（归一化、SMOTE平衡、PCA变换、SMOTE+PCA组合）下训练KNN、随机森林和SVM三种机器学习模型，并以精确率、召回率和F1分数评估性能，优先关注严重故障类别。

Result: 随机森林在IEEE-30上取得最高F1分数0.97，在IEEE-14上为0.86；SVM从PCA中获益显著并提高了分类准确率；KNN最适合SMOTE和PCA转换；PCA对模型整体性能的贡献大于SMOTE；SMOTE虽能提高召回率但可能引入误报，是对准确率的折衷。

Conclusion: 本研究强调机器学习可作为传统故障分析的强大且可扩展的替代方案，有助于提升电力系统安全性的实时评估能力。

Abstract: Ensuring the security of the power system is essential for stability and reliability, especially in the event of disruption. Effective classification of contingency in power systems enables proactive decision-making and mitigates large-scale breakdowns and failures. This study explores the use of machine learning algorithms to classify security levels of contingencies in power systems into safe, moderate or severe classes. For this approach, Newton-Raphson load flow method extracts system data from contingency scenarios, using Overall Performance Index (OPI) as safety measure. For data pre-processing, Synthetic Minority Over-Sampling Technique (SMOTE) and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is used to address class imbalance and reduce dimensionality, respectively. K-Nearest Neighbours (KNN), Random Forest (RF) and Support Vector Machines (SVM) is trained and evaluated on datasets generated through N-k contingency scenarios for k equal 1, 2, and 3 on IEEE-14 and IEEE-30 bus systems using four hybrid pre-processing configurations: normalized, SMOTE-balanced, PCA-transformed, and a combined SMOTE PCA-transformed. Performance is assessed by precision, recall and F1 score, with priority given to the severe contingency classes. The RF achieved the highest F1 scores of 0.97 in IEEE-30 and 0.86 in IEEE-14, SVM benefits significantly from PCA and improves the accuracy of the classification, while KNN is best suited for SMOTE and PCA conversion. The findings show that PCA contributes more than SMOTE to the overall performance of the model. However, SMOTE improves recall but can introduce false positives and is therefore a compromise of accuracy. This study highlights machine learning as a scalable and powerful alternative to traditional contingency analysis, which improves the assessment of security in real time.

</details>


### [149] [Iris: Climbing to the Search Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04304)
*Ziyuan Liu,Hengqi Liu,Zichuan Wang,Yang Qin,Jiachen Liang,Xu Chu,Shaowei Chen,Yuantao Gu,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Iris-mini（35B-A3B）和Iris-pro（397B-A17B）两个搜索智能体，并公布了其背后的数据流水线与训练配方。通过从网页超链接结构中反向构建多跳问题，生成轨迹后进行SFT，再通过RL对实时搜索进行优化，采用名为"SFT-RL climbing"的两阶段交替训练流程。在BrowseComp、BrowseComp-ZH、DeepSearchQA和HLE基准上，两个模型分别达到82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3和88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4的成绩，是其参数范围内开源搜索智能体的最强结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的搜索智能体在训练数据构建、训练流程设计上缺少系统性方法，且推理时上下文管理对基准性能的影响往往被忽视或与系统间差异混淆。作者希望构建一个完整可复现的数据流水线和训练配方，两阶段交替训练出强搜索智能体，并严格隔离推理时上下文管理的变量影响，使结果具备可比性和可复现性。

Method: 1) 数据构建：从网页超链接结构中反向构建多跳问题链，将非答案实体改写为描述性引用以防止字符串匹配解题，仅保留参考模型闭卷无法解答但提供证据后可解的题目。2) 将问题转化为搜索轨迹，在轨迹级和回合级双重过滤后用于SFT。3) 在实时搜索环境下用RL优化策略，奖励判断与观察摘要器部署于训练集群内，过长回放在请求级中断并在下一步从已提交前缀恢复。4) 采用SFT-RL climbing交替训练：将每轮RL中最难解决且最高效的回放回传给下一轮监督训练。5) 推理时上下文管理作为固定变量在所有基准上开/关进行评估，工具集、上下文限制和评估器保持固定，使用单一ReAct智能体，无子智能体和测试时验证。

Result: 在启用上下文管理时，Iris-mini在BrowseComp/BrowseComp-ZH/DeepSearchQA/HLE上分别达到82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3，Iris-pro达到88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4，均为各自参数范围内开源搜索智能体的最强综合表现。模型权重连同完整的数据构建、训练和评估配方将公开发布。

Conclusion: Iris-mini和Iris-pro证明了大规模结合数据工程、两阶段交替训练（SFT-RL climbing）与推理时上下文管理可以显著提升搜索智能体的性能。同时作者指出推理时上下文管理在基准上的贡献比多数系统间差异更有价值，强调了公平评估上下文管理影响的重要性，并承诺开源模型权重与完整配方以促进可复现研究。

Abstract: We present Iris-mini and Iris-pro, two search agents trained at the 35B-A3B and 397B-A17B scales, together with the data pipeline and training recipe behind them. Tasks are reverse-constructed from the hyperlink structure of a web corpus: we author multi-hop chains over an entity graph distilled from a seed page and its out-links, rewrite every non-answer entity into a descriptive reference so that no clue can be resolved by string matching, and admit only questions that a reference model fails closed-book yet solves once the supporting evidence is supplied. These questions are then turned into trajectories, which are filtered at both the trajectory and the turn level before SFT. The policy is then optimized by RL against live search, with the reward judge and the observation summarizer served inside the training cluster, and with over-long rollouts interrupted at the request level and resumed from their committed prefix at the next step. We alternate the two stages in a procedure we call SFT-RL climbing, returning the hardest solved and most efficient rollouts of each RL round to the next supervised pass. Because inference-time context management is worth more on these benchmarks than most reported differences between systems, we evaluate every benchmark both with and without it, holding the tool set, the context limit, and the judge fixed. All results come from a single ReAct agent, with no sub-agents and no test-time verification. With management enabled, on BrowseComp, BrowseComp-ZH, DeepSearchQA, and HLE the two models reach $82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3$ and $88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4$, the strongest overall results among open-source search agents in their respective parameter ranges. We plan to release the model weights together with the complete recipe for data construction, training, and evaluation.

</details>


### [150] [A Removal Based Approach to Improve LLM Faithfulness at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04343)
*Qinglan Luo,S M A Nahian,John Guttag,S. Mazdak Abulnaga,Katie Matton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种测试时（test-time）方法来改善大语言模型（LLM）解释的忠实性（faithfulness），特别针对"不完整性"（incompleteness）问题：从输入中移除解释中未提及的概念后重新查询模型，从而消除隐藏影响。该方法无需修改模型参数，跨两个数据集、多个模型家族和两个独立忠实性指标均优于标准提示和鼓励忠实性的提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多地用于高影响的决策，其解释成为审计模型行为的重要工具。然而现有的解释可能不忠实（unfaithful），无法反映模型实际推理过程。已有方法要么需要训练访问权（训练时方法），要么主要关注"不健全性"（unsoundness）问题而忽视"不完整性"（incompleteness）问题。作者旨在提供一种无需权重访问、直接针对不完整性问题的测试时方法。

Method: 作者将解释不忠实划分为两个维度：不完整性（省略影响答案的因素）和不健全性（引用了未影响答案的因素）。提出的方法为：从输入中移除解释中未归因（uncredited）的概念，然后在缩减后的输入上重新查询模型。这样消除了未提及的影响，同时保留了已提及概念的影响。该方法可以在推理时应用，无需修改模型参数，是模型无关的。

Result: 在跨两个数据集、多个模型家族以及两个独立忠实性指标的评估中，该方法相比标准提示（standard prompting）和鼓励忠实性的提示（prompting to encourage faithfulness）均提升了解释忠实性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种灵活、模型无关的测试时机制来减少隐藏影响，改善了LLM辅助决策的可靠性和安全性，补充了现有主要针对不健全性的方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for consequential decisions, making their explanations an important tool for auditing model behavior. Unfortunately, these explanations can be unfaithful, failing to reflect the actual reasoning underlying the model's decisions. We consider a setting in which an LLM provides both an answer and an explanation in response to a question. We identify two distinct dimensions of unfaithful explanations: incompleteness, meaning that the explanation omits factors that influence the answer, and unsoundness, meaning that the explanation cites factors that did not influence the model's answer. Existing approaches to improving LLM faithfulness include training-time methods, which require access to model weights and extensive computational resources, and test-time methods that largely focus on addressing unsoundness. We introduce a test-time approach that directly targets incompleteness. We remove from the input the concepts not credited in the model's explanation and re-query the model on the reduced input. This eliminates unmentioned influences while preserving the influence of mentioned concepts. Across two datasets, multiple model families, and two independent faithfulness metrics, our approach improves explanation faithfulness compared to both standard prompting and prompting to encourage faithfulness. Our method is model-agnostic and can be applied at inference time without modifying model parameters, providing a flexible mechanism for reducing hidden influences and improving the reliability and safety of LLM-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [151] [Why Better Models Can Create Riskier Systems: Evidence from LLM Agents in Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04373)
*Jillian Ross,Eric So,Zoe De Simone,Charles Pozniak,Andrew W. Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型语言模型的一个"能力悖论"：提升个体模型能力反而可能降低系统级表现。因为共享训练数据和架构使更强大的LLM行为更为相似，产生无法多样化消除的相关性行动，从而形成不可分散的风险底线。作者通过金融市场中的代理模拟实验验证了这一理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被大规模部署于金融、内容审核、招聘等关键现实系统中，但这些系统的鲁棒性不仅取决于单个模型的能力，还取决于系统内多个模型行为的组合特性。现有研究主要关注提升单模型能力，忽视了多个LLM协同部署时可能产生的系统性风险。

Method: 作者首先提出一个通用理论框架，阐述共享训练和架构导致更强大LLM行为更趋同、形成不可分散风险底线的机制；随后在金融市场中使用包含不同通用能力LLM交易者的基于代理的模拟进行检验。模拟涵盖多种场景以分离准确性收益与相关性风险的影响。

Result: 实验发现：（1）前沿LLM表现出显著的相关行为，且随能力提升而增强；（2）当共享推理准确时，增加代理参与度可降低市场级风险；（3）当代理共享共同错误信息环境时，相同的相关行为成为负担。这些结果共同验证了能力悖论的存在。

Conclusion: 提高个体模型能力并不必然带来更好的系统级结果。模型能力提升带来的准确性增益可能被相关性增强所抵消甚至反转。该结论在其他领域是否适用是一个待验证的开放实证问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being deployed at scale in consequential real-world systems, from financial markets to content moderation to hiring. We show that improving individual model capability can degrade rather than improve system-level outcomes. We hypothesize that shared training and architectures can lead more capable LLMs to behave more similarly, creating correlated actions that do not diversify away. We develop a general framework showing how this correlation creates a non-diversifiable risk floor and test its predictions in financial markets using an agent-based simulation with LLM traders of varying general-purpose capability. We find that: (1) frontier LLMs exhibit significantly correlated behavior that increases with capability; (2) when their shared reasoning is accurate, increasing agent participation reduces market-level risk; and (3) when agents share a common misinformation environment, the same correlated behavior becomes a liability. Together, these results identify a capability paradox: improving individual models does not necessarily produce better system-level outcomes. Whether the same dynamics arise in other domains is an open empirical question.

</details>


### [152] [Corporate Language Model (CLM): Transforming Tacit and Fragmented Enterprise Knowledge into a Sovereign, Auditable, and Executable Corporate Intelligence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04377)
*Fabricio C. Avini,Guilherme Trez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Corporate Language Model (CLM)框架，将企业知识转化为基于本体的企业基础，包含五个能力平面和四个架构支柱，实现企业AI的可推理与可治理执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI部署失败常源于缺乏承载决策、协商与执行的结构化基底，而非模型自身能力不足。通用LLM缺乏企业特定本体先验，RAG脆弱且无法通向可执行动作，静态剧本无法推理与适应，因此需要将隐性知识捕获、本体锚定、主权部署与可审计执行协同设计的新架构。

Method: CLM框架由五个能力平面和四个架构支柱构成：神经符号网耦合生成模型与知识图谱；技能图（Skill Graph）对可复用战术、角色、异议与目标进行类型化与组合；活体数字孪生将功能域建模为推理替身；深度安全层确保主权、可追踪性与人类监督。采用Spec-as-Code范式桥接具备本体的意图与可执行制品。

Result: CLM提供四项贡献：将CLM定义为一类独特研究客体；提出技能图实现可解释性的即构式组合；提出"智慧倾听者"效应——具备隐性能力的基础随使用而价值复合增长，连接动态能力与组织学习；在巴西一家JCI认证的三级医院中，LGPD合规情境下实例化了六个成熟度阶段中的三个阶段。

Conclusion: CLM是基础中心型架构类的一种实例化，通过知识即基础的方式弥合了企业大语言模型从"生成式问答"到"可治理执行"的鸿沟，并以医疗机构的实际部署证明了其可行性与阶段可达性。

Abstract: Enterprise AI deployments fail not from model inadequacy, but because organizations lack a structured substrate encoding how they decide, negotiate, and execute. Generic LLMs carry no firm-specific ontological priors; RAG remains brittle, with no path to executable action; static playbooks encode logic but cannot reason or adapt. This demands an architecture treating tacit-knowledge capture, ontological grounding, sovereign deployment, and auditable actuation as co-designed from the start. This paper introduces the Corporate Language Model (CLM), a framework transforming a firm's structured, unstructured, multimodal, and tacit knowledge into an ontology-grounded enterprise foundation upon which reasoning and governed execution are composed. CLM has five capability planes and four architectural pillars: a Neurosymbolic Mesh coupling generative models with a knowledge graph; a Skill Graph where reusable tactics, personas, objections, and goals are typed and composed; Living Digital Twins modeling functional areas as reasoning surrogates; and a Deep Security Layer enforcing sovereignty, traceability, and human oversight. A Spec-as-Code paradigm bridges grounded intent and executable artifact. CLM is one instantiation of this foundation-centric class. Four contributions follow: CLM is defined as a distinct object of study; the Skill Graph is introduced for compositional explainability by construction; the Wisdom Listener effect is proposed, whereby tacit-capable foundations compound in value with use, connecting to dynamic capabilities and organizational learning; and evidence from a JCI-accredited tertiary hospital in Brazil instantiates three of the six maturity stages under LGPD.

</details>


### [153] [HarvestBench: Measuring Whether LLM Agents Will Pay to Avoid Killing Animals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04444)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Miles Tidmarsh,Matthias Endres,Anshuman Singh,Jeremiah Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarvestBench是首个为"避免副作用"定价、并把副作用明确定义为活物的基准测试。它是一个农场模拟环境：LLM子代理操控两辆拖拉机协同收割玉米，田地中有动物。作者用七千多个带价格决策衡量模型愿意付出多少燃料成本来绕开动物（而非无生命的岩石和干草捆），发现各模型杀生率差异巨大（0.4%~98.8%）但与能力无关，部分模型对价格敏感，道德叙事的提示词对结果影响最大，且该基准完全可复现、无需LLM打分器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基准评估智能体达成目标过程中的副作用，但没有一个把"避免副作用"标上价格、且将副作用明确视为有生命的生物。本研究旨在衡量模型在真实代价（而非口头表态）下会为不伤害生物付出多少，从而弥补现有评估只测"模型怎么说"而不测"模型愿意付多少去避免伤害"的空白。

Method: 构建一个无记忆的强化学习网格世界农场模拟。LLM子代理控制两辆拖拉机协作收割，遇到动物挡路时，自动驾驶停车询问模型：直接碾过（无燃油成本）或绕行（付出标价燃油）。对照组为岩石（毁坏拖拉机）和干草捆（无害且非活物），另设从邻居田地偷取作物的第二重道德测试。通过游戏日志计数得分，无需LLM评判器。

Result: 9个模型共7,201次定价决策，其中3,951次涉及动物。碾杀率从0.4%到98.8%不等，Terra和Sol最仁慈，GPT-4o-mini最残忍，且排序与模型能力无关。6个模型中有4个对价格显著敏感（弹性0.09~1.69）。所有9个模型在默认地图上碾过野生动物的频率高于农场动物。道德提示词影响最大：6个推理模型在道德提示下5个碾杀率低于6%，移除提示后6个全部超过84%。

Conclusion: HarvestBench通过给回避伤害定价，衡量的是模型"愿意付出什么代价避免伤害"而非"如何谈论伤害"。结果表明各模型在避免伤害上的行为差异巨大且与能力无关；价格敏感性普遍存在但程度不同；野生物与养殖物被区别对待；最关键的仍是提示词（道德叙事）的设定。该基准用游戏日志计分、无LLM评判器，因此完全可复现。

Abstract: Benchmarks for the side effects an agent causes on the way to a goal already exist, but HarvestBench is the first to put a price on avoiding the side effect and to name that side effect as a living creature. It is a farm simulation: LLM sub-agents drive a crew of two tractors through a cooperative corn harvest, with animals in the field. The environment is a reinforcement learning gridworld, every decision is made without memory, and the harm is never named in the goal. When an animal blocks a tractor's route the autopilot stops and asks the model whether to drive on, at no fuel cost, or swerve around it for a posted fuel price. Kills are compared against two controls: rocks, which damage the tractor and are hit under 1% of the time by every model, and hay bales, which are harmless and not alive. Models can also take crops from the neighbor's field instead of their own, a second test of what they treat as moral.
  Across nine models and 7,201 priced decisions, 3,951 involved an animal rather than a hay bale or a rock. Kill rates range from 0.4% to 98.8%, with Terra and Sol the most merciful and GPT-4o-mini the most cruel, and they are not ordered by capability. Four of six models were sensitive to price at the 5% level, with elasticities from 0.09 to 1.69. All nine drove over wild animals more often than farmed animals on the default map, and the direction held at every map geometry in every model with room to move. The briefing mattered most: under the morality briefing the kill rate was under 6% in five of six reasoning models, and removing it raised the kill rate above 84% in all six.
  HarvestBench uses no LLM grader. The scorer counts events in the game log, so it is fully reproducible, and it measures what a model will pay to avoid harm rather than what it says about harm.

</details>


### [154] [PerfReasoning: How Well Do LLMs Reason on Hardware Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04476)
*Dan Zhao,Karthikeyan Sankaralingam,Christos Kozyrakis,Qijing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PerfReasoning基准，用于评估LLM在硬件性能建模方面的能力，涵盖直接性能推理与生成分析性性能模型代码两个层面。结果显示闭源最强模型在推理问答上超过90%，开源最佳达82.4%，但模型构建（代码生成）难度显著更大，除GPT-5.6 Sol超过80%外，其他模型平均低于15%且运行间差异大。任务特定RL可将4B模型的映射推理准确率提升15.7个百分点，而免反馈多轮自修订提示不够可靠。基准将公开以支持可复现评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能建模对硬件设计与软件优化至关重要，但构建此类模型需要对计算、数据复用、存储与移动进行结构化推理。现有LLM在此领域的系统评估尚属空白，作者期望构建基准来揭示LLM在架构推理与性能模型构建之间的能力差距。

Method: 提出PerfReasoning基准，给定工作负载、架构与映射规格，要求模型（1）比较不同映射方案并预测片外流量与缓冲区需求（直接性能推理），（2）生成分析性性能模型代码。评估覆盖多种闭源与开源模型，并测试任务特定RL训练与免反馈多轮自修订提示策略对模型性能的提升效果。

Result: 最强闭源模型（如GPT-5.6系列）在推理型问答上超过90%准确率，最佳开源权重模型达82.4%；但在性能模型代码生成上，除GPT-5.6 Sol超过80%通过率外，其余模型配置平均低于15%且跨运行波动显著。任务特定RL将4B模型的映射推理准确率提升15.7个点，而免反馈多轮自修订提示未能稳定有效。

Conclusion: PerfReasoning揭示了LLM在看似合理的架构推理与可靠的性能模型构建之间存在显著鸿沟。基准将公开发布，以支持可复现的评估并追踪该领域的后续进展。

Abstract: Performance modeling is central to hardware design and software optimization, yet constructing these models requires structured reasoning about computation, data reuse, storage, and movement. We introduce PerfReasoning, a benchmark that evaluates LLMs both as direct performance reasoners and as generators of analytical performance-model code. Given workload, architecture, and mapping specifications, models compare mappings and predict off-chip traffic and buffer requirements. The strongest closed-source models exceed 90% on reasoning-based Q&A, and the best open-weight model reaches 82.4%. However, model construction is substantially harder: while GPT-5.6 Sol exceeds 80% pass rate, all other model configurations average below 15% and vary markedly across runs. Task-specific RL raises a 4B model's mapping-reasoning accuracy by 15.7 points, whereas feedback-free multi-round self-revision prompting is not reliably effective. PerfReasoning exposes the gap between plausible architectural reasoning and reliable performance-model construction. We will publicly release the benchmark to support reproducible evaluation and track future progress.

</details>


### [155] [When Quantization Breaks Memory: Recurrent-State Write-Back in Low-Precision Temporal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04490)
*Ismail Erbas,Xavier Intes,Vikas Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"recurrent-state write-back"（循环状态回写）概念，指代量化循环网络中状态存储规则对后续计算的影响。在用于荧光寿命成像的紧凑GRU编码器-解码器中，将连续状态传播替换为4-bit确定性状态存储会使τ1和τ2估计误差分别增加约70倍和300倍。错误反馈、残差记忆和方向记忆可跨时间传递被抑制更新的信息，无需重训练即可恢复精度。该现象在LSTM中同样复现，且细胞状态比隐藏状态对量化更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化在循环神经网络推理中虽能降低计算和内存开销，但量化状态被存储并在下一时间步返回，存储规则可能改变后续计算。这种效应此前未被系统研究，理解它对于量化循环推断的设计至关重要。

Method: 在紧凑GRU编码器-解码器（用于荧光寿命成像）中，固定训练好的模型，将连续状态传播替换为确定性4-bit状态存储，量化误差影响。引入错误反馈、残差记忆和方向记忆机制传递被抑制更新的信息。进行精度扫描以考察状态精度对固定循环解的影响，并通过匹配训练测试状态接口兼容性是否可学习。最后在独立训练的LSTM上重复干预实验验证泛化性。

Result: 4-bit状态存储使τ1和τ2估计误差分别增加约70倍和300倍。失败原因在于重复的小更新低于写入阈值，使存储状态几乎固定而网络仍持续提出变化。错误反馈、残差记忆和方向记忆可恢复精度。提高状态精度可能恶化固定循环解，但匹配训练可学习状态接口兼容性。LSTM上复现了同样失败模式，错误反馈可恢复精度，且细胞状态比隐藏状态更敏感。

Conclusion: 循环状态回写是低精度循环动力学的关键决定因素，状态存储接口是量化循环推断的核心设计考量。

Abstract: Quantization is widely used to reduce the computational and memory demands of neural-network inference. In recurrent networks, however, the quantized state is stored and returned at the next time step, so the rule used to store that state can alter subsequent computations. Here, we introduce recurrent-state write-back to denote this rule and isolate its effect in a compact GRU encoder--decoder for fluorescence lifetime imaging, a molecular imaging modality used in quantitative biological imaging. A central task is estimating two lifetime parameters, the short-lived component τ1 and the long-lived component τ2, from high-noise time-resolved fluorescence signals. Holding the trained model fixed, replacing continuous state propagation with deterministic 4-bit state storage increases estimation errors for τ1 and τ2 by approximately 70x and 300x, respectively. Failure occurs when repeated small updates remain below the write threshold, leaving the stored state nearly fixed while the network continues to propose change. Error feedback, residual memory, and direction memory carry information from these suppressed updates across time and recover accuracy without retraining. Precision sweeps show that increasing state precision can worsen a fixed recurrent solution, while matched training shows that compatibility with the state interface can be learned. To test whether this behavior extends beyond the GRU, we repeat the post-training intervention in an independently trained LSTM, where coarse write-back reproduces the failure, error feedback restores accuracy, and state-specific interventions reveal greater sensitivity of the cell state than the hidden state. Our results establish recurrent-state write-back as a key determinant of low-precision recurrent dynamics and identify the state-storage interface as a central design consideration for quantized recurrent inference.

</details>


### [156] [ResLearn-XR: Residual Learning for Network Traffic and Quality-of-Experience-Aware Modeling in Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04493)
*Yoga Suhas Kuruba Manjunath,Jie Gao,Lian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ResLearn-XR，一种用于XR网络流量预测与QoE风险评估的残差学习框架，采用两阶段时序学习结构（基础序列预测+任务特定残差学习），在值空间进行连续流量预测、在logit空间进行概率QoE风险估计，并通过Data Descriptor Algorithm (DDA)实现帧时序感知的特征构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR网络流量具有突发性、非平稳性等动态特征，传统单阶段预测模型难以在连续流量预测和QoE风险评估两大任务上同时取得良好性能，需要能够自适应捕捉XR流量动态并可靠估计用户体验质量风险的方法。

Method: ResLearn-XR采用两阶段时序学习结构：第一阶段为基础序列预测模型，第二阶段引入任务特定的残差学习组件。流量预测在值空间进行，QoE风险估计在logit空间进行。同时引入Data Descriptor Algorithm (DDA)，一种因果特征构建模块，将应用层可观测数据转换为帧时序感知描述符以支持加密流量分析；并构建了配对连续XR流量轨迹与会话级用户上报QoE标签的数据集。

Result: 在帧计数、帧大小和到达间隔时间预测上SMAPE最多降低17.84%；在QoE风险评估上与单阶段基线相比SMAPE最多降低87.8%。

Conclusion: ResLearn-XR通过残差学习增强基础序列模型的自适应能力，有效提升了XR流量预测精度与QoE风险估计性能，验证了在值空间与logit空间分别进行残差学习的有效性，并为加密XR流量分析提供了可行的特征构造方案。

Abstract: We present ResLearn-XR, a residual learning framework for predicting eXtended Reality (XR) network traffic and estimating Quality-of-Experience (QoE) risk. ResLearn-XR adopts a two-stage temporal learning structure comprising a base sequence prediction model augmented with task-specific residual learning components to improve adaptability to bursty, non-stationary XR traffic dynamics. The residual learning stages operate in the value space for continuous XR traffic forecasting and in the logit space for probabilistic QoE risk estimation. \rev{For the QoE-risk branch, we introduce a Data Descriptor Algorithm (DDA), a causal feature-construction module that converts packet-level application-layer observables into frame-timing-aware descriptors suitable for encrypted traffic analysis. We also construct an XR Traffic-QoE dataset that pairs continuous XR traffic traces with session-level user-reported QoE labels. ResLearn-XR reduces SMAPE by up to 17.84% across frame-count, frame-size, and inter-arrival-time prediction, while reducing QoE-risk estimation SMAPE by up to 87.8% over single-stage baselines.

</details>


### [157] [Rethinking Indirect Prompt Injection as a Test-Time Search Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04495)
*Duong M. Nguyen,Joon Sik Kim,Blazej Manczak,Vaikkunth Mugunthan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文档提出了一个针对工具使用型智能体的间接提示注入攻击框架，将攻击建模为对任务相关攻击面的测试时搜索，并引入了一个具有专用搜索机制的智能体攻击器。研究发现增加攻击者的测试时计算量能提升漏洞发现与利用能力，显式策略管理对避免冗余搜索至关重要。结果表明智能体安全评估应同时刻画攻击者的搜索过程和计算预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间接提示注入对工具使用型智能体构成重大安全威胁，但现有研究通常将攻击成功视为受害者模型的预算无关属性，缺乏对攻击者搜索过程和计算资源的系统性刻画。作者希望形式化这种攻击并探究攻击者自适应搜索计算量对漏洞利用的影响。

Method: 将间接提示注入形式化为对环境、用户任务和注入任务共同诱导的任务相关攻击面上的测试时搜索。引入一个配备专用搜索机制的智能体攻击器，该机制包括环境侦察、基于结构化推理的攻击策略规划和利用受害者代理反馈的自适应评估。通过跨异构任务进行实验，包括增加攻击者测试时计算预算的扩展实验及消融研究。

Result: 跨异构任务的结果显示，增加攻击者的测试时计算量能够改善漏洞发现和利用的成功率。消融实验表明，显式的策略管理在避免冗余搜索和在大计算预算下持续取得收益方面至关重要。

Conclusion: 智能体安全评估应同时表征攻击者的搜索过程与计算预算，而不应将攻击成功率视为受害者模型的预算无关属性。攻击者对系统攻击面的自适应搜索是工具使用型智能体面临的重要且尚未充分探索的安全风险。

Abstract: We formulate indirect prompt injection as a test-time search over a task-dependent attack surface induced by the environment, user task, and injection task. To operationalize this formulation, we introduce an agentic attacker with a dedicated search harness that performs environment reconnaissance, structured reasoning over attack strategies, and adaptive evaluation using victim-agent feedback. Across heterogeneous tasks, we find that increasing attacker test-time compute improves vulnerability discovery and exploitation, while ablations show that explicit strategy management is important for avoiding redundant search and sustaining gains at larger budgets. These results suggest that agentic security evaluations should characterize both the attacker's search procedure and compute budget, rather than treating attack success as a budget-independent property of the victim. More broadly, our findings identify the attacker's adaptive search over the system attack surfaces as an important and underexplored security risk for tool-using agents.

</details>


### [158] [BioSync: Transformer-Based Cross-Modal Fusion for a Multimodal Physiological Digital Biomarker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04504)
*Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioSync提出了一种连续复合数字生物标志物（BSI），通过多头自注意力融合可穿戴与移动设备采集的多模态生理测量数据（HRV、EEG、活动记录和语音等），并在线性分支中保留标准特征拼接能力。在认知衰退队列中AUC达0.928，在代谢-自主神经队列中准确率/F1为0.764/0.766，BSI与潜在严重程度的相关系数分别为0.91和0.68，总体上略优于简单特征拼接及纯注意力变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴和移动设备采集的心脏、神经、行为和语音等测量仅提供部分且含噪的生理状态视图，单一模态信息不完整。现有方法未能充分捕获跨模态联合观测中可能存在的互补信息，因此需要一种能整合多模态数据、基于BEST框架定义连续复合数字生物标志物的方法。

Method: BioSync采用多头自注意力机制处理各模态token，同时增加一个假设类包含标准特征拼接的线性分支，形成wide与deep相结合的架构。该设计受潜变量测量理论与"联合观测可能包含单模态无法获得的信息"这一可能性的启发。在训练中采用模态dropout策略增强鲁棒性，并在合成队列上验证。

Result: 在四模态认知衰退队列中，BioSync的AUC为0.928，特征拼接为0.926；在代谢队列中准确率/F1分别为0.764/0.766对比0.756/0.758。BSI与潜在严重程度相关性r=0.91和0.68。纯注意力消融在认知队列仅获0.911，表明性能增益来自wide-and-deep组合架构。在模态dropout匹配训练下，BioSync在六种认知队列腐败率中的五种及最高代谢腐败率下优于拼接，AUC也高于五个已发表数字生物标志物参考值。

Conclusion: BioSync在合成队列上的表现表明其多模态融合架构相对特征拼接、单模态及纯注意力等方法具有潜在优势，尤其在数据缺失/噪声条件下的鲁棒性更佳。但当前验证局限于文献信息构建的合成队列，且数据集和任务差异限制了与已发表参考值的受控比较，未来需要在真实队列中进一步验证其计算属性与临床应用价值。

Abstract: Cardiac, neural, behavioral, and speech measurements from wearable and mobile devices provide partial, noise-sensitive views of physiological state. BioSync combines these measurements into the \textbf{BioSync Index (BSI)}, a continuous composite digital biomarker defined under the BEST framework. The model applies multi-head self-attention to modality tokens and adds a linear branch whose hypothesis class includes standard feature concatenation. This architecture is motivated by latent-variable measurement theory and by the possibility that joint observations contain information unavailable from individual modalities. We evaluated BioSync on two literature-informed synthetic cohorts: a four-modality cognitive-decline cohort using HRV, EEG, actigraphy, and speech, and a metabolic-autonomic cohort structured around the public AI-READI wearable schema. In the cognitive cohort, BioSync and concatenation obtained AUCs of 0.928 and 0.926, respectively. In the metabolic cohort, BioSync obtained accuracy/F1 of 0.764/0.766, compared with 0.756/0.758 for concatenation. The BSI correlated with latent severity in both cohorts ($r=0.91$ and $r=0.68$). A pure-attention ablation obtained cognitive-cohort AUC 0.911, locating the increase to 0.928 in the combined wide-and-deep architecture. With matched modality-dropout training, BioSync led concatenation at five of six cognitive-cohort corruption rates and at the highest metabolic-cohort rate. Its cognitive-cohort AUC was also higher than five published digital-biomarker reference values, although differences in datasets and tasks preclude a controlled benchmark claim. Comparison with single-modality, early-fusion, and late-fusion designs across six prespecified criteria identifies the model's computational properties; validation on real cohorts remains necessary.

</details>


### [159] [What Does Multi-Harness RL Learn? Credit Assignment and Portability in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04518)
*Chenqian Le,Jiayi Cheng,Qijia He,Runhao Li,Yinghao Li,Xupeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在仓库级代码生成任务中，通过大规模受控实验（24,000次密封评估）隔离研究了多harness强化学习中"相对优势分组规则"的影响。结果显示：评估harness的选择是主导变量（均值解法率从2.14%到9.27%，相差4.3倍），而训练配方只影响1.16；分组规则（Within vs Cross）差异在统计上不显著（Cross减Within仅+0.25 pp，置信区间跨零），且种子间波动大于组间差异。Cross规则的优势主要携带harness特定信息（分类器可恢复生成harness），并非更可迁移的能力。结论强调多harness RL报告应明确分组边界，并在未见过的harness上测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多harness强化学习配方包含两个选择：将策略暴露于多个harness，以及在同一相对优势组内比较它们的奖励。现有研究常混淆这两个因素，作者希望隔离验证"分组规则"（Within vs Cross）这一单独选择的真实效果，探究跨harness信用分配是否真的能带来比harness内信用更强的可迁移能力。

Method: 从Qwen3-8B监督热启动出发，在固定任务-harness记录（来自Aider、OpenHands、Qwen Code、SWE-agent四种源harness）上重放相同的冻结数据和更新次数，对比两种GRPO分组规则：Within（每任务-harness对一组）与Cross（同一任务内harness池化）。用密封的SWE-bench Verified oracle对每个检查点在四个源harness及一个训练中未见的极简harness上评分，并通过分类器、置信区间、种子方差及on-policy数据重采集进行多角度验证。

Result: 评估harness主导结果：使均值解法率从2.14%变为9.27%（4.3倍），训练配方仅影响1.16。分组规则无显著差异：在held-out harness上Cross减Within为+0.25 pp（95% CI [-0.48, +1.02]），多种子池化后+0.16 pp（[-0.41, +0.72]），且各规则种子范围（0.42-0.45 pp）超过组间差异。分类器能从Cross的优势（+4.48 pp）中恢复生成harness，从Within中完全不能，但两种规则在held-out harness上得分和动作分布相同。on-policy重采集不改变结论。

Conclusion: 跨harness信用（Cross规则）带来的只是配置适配（对训练时所用harness的针对性优化），而非比harness内信用更可迁移的能力。研究者报告多harness RL结果时应明确说明分组边界，并使用训练中未见过的评估harness进行测试，否则可能将harness特定信号误认为泛化能力。

Abstract: Agent reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly runs through full execution harnesses, and a multi-harness recipe mixes two choices: exposing the policy to several harnesses, and comparing their rewards inside one relative-advantage group. We isolate the second choice in repository-level coding. From one Qwen3-8B supervised warm start we replay the same frozen task-harness records from Aider, OpenHands, Qwen Code, and SWE-agent, with the same number of updates, under two rules for group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), Within (one group per task-harness pair) and Cross (harnesses pooled within a task), and score every checkpoint with a sealed SWE-bench Verified oracle on four source harnesses and a minimal harness held out of training. The evaluation harness is the dominant variable: across 24,000 sealed evaluations it moves the mean solve rate from 2.14\% to 9.27\%, a factor of 4.3, where the training recipe moves it by 1.16. The grouping rule is not. On the held-out harness, Cross minus Within is +0.25 pp, 95\% confidence interval [-0.48, +1.02], at eight attempts per task, and +0.16 [-0.41, +0.72] pooled over three training seeds whose individual estimates change sign. Each rule's own seed range, 0.42 to 0.45 pp, exceeds the difference between them. Both rules place their largest gains on the same source harness. The pooled advantage carries the harness: an out-of-fold classifier recovers the generating harness from Cross's advantage +4.48 pp above the shuffled-label baseline and from Within's not at all, and the two rules still reach the same held-out score and action distribution inside each harness. Re-collecting half the training data on-policy does not change this. Cross-harness credit yields configuration adaptation and no more portable capability than within-harness credit. Multi-harness RL reports should state the grouping boundary and test under an unseen harness.

</details>


### [160] [MaxKernel: Agentic Kernel Generation for TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04523)
*Shangkun Wang,Nina Cai,Charles Hoong,Julian Walker,Gerson Kroiz,George Vanica,Deepak Patil,Andi Gavrilescu,Hassan Sipra,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MaxKernel是一个多智能体系统，通过三种范式（人机协作、全自动优化、基于图的自动搜索）结合LLM和实时编译器反馈，实现TPU高效内核的自动化生成与优化，在50个多样化内核任务基准上达到专家手工调优的性能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为加速器编写高性能自定义内核需要深厚的硬件专业知识，传统上依赖专家手工实现，工作量大且难以扩展。利用大语言模型结合真实编译器反馈来构建自主智能体系统，有望自动化这一复杂过程。

Method: MaxKernel实现了三种内核开发范式：(1) 人机协作(HITL)智能体支持分步协作设计；(2) 自主(Auto)智能体执行全自动的基于指标/轨迹的优化循环；(3) 基于图的自主搜索扩展Auto智能体以进行全局设计空间探索。三种范式共享一组专门的子智能体池，分别负责规划、实现、自我调试、测试和硬件性能分析。

Result: 在JaxBench基准（涵盖50个多样化TPU内核任务）以及来自最新开源模型的复杂真实负载上，MaxKernel一致地生成高度优化的实现，与专家手工调优的基线相当，并在基准上取得显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: MaxKernel证明了基于LLM的多智能体系统结合编译器反馈能够达到专家级TPU内核开发水平，且系统已开源，为自动化硬件内核工程提供了可行且可复现的解决方案。

Abstract: Designing and authoring high-performance custom kernels for accelerators is a complex task that requires deep hardware-level expertise. Large Language Models (LLM) can be leveraged together with real-time compiler feedback to build agentic systems for kernel generation. In this work, we present MaxKernel, a multi-agent system that implements three distinct paradigms for TPU kernel development: (1) a Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) agent for collaborative, step-by-step design; (2) an Autonomous (Auto) agent that executes a fully automated, metric/trace-driven optimization loop; and (3) a Graph-Based Autonomous Search that scales the Auto agent for global exploration of the design space. All three paradigms leverage a shared pool of specialized sub-agents to handle planning, implementation, self-debugging, testing, and hardware profiling. We evaluate MaxKernel on JaxBench, a comprehensive suite of 50 diverse kernel tasks for TPUs, alongside complex, real-world workloads from state-of-the-art open-source models. We demonstrate that MaxKernel consistently generates highly optimized implementations, matching expert hand-tuned baselines and delivering significant performance across the benchmark. Our agent is open-sourced and available https://github.com/AI-Hypercomputer/accelerator-agents/tree/main/MaxKernel.

</details>


### [161] [Towards a universal language of concepts: A survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04528)
*Aishni Parab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出程序是概念通用表示的有力候选，并回顾了使用程序作为概念表示的多种计算模型，评估它们对构建通用表示语言的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够从稀疏数据中学习和泛化新概念，原因在于其以丰富的结构化格式表达知识。本文希望探究一种能够普遍表示概念的语言，而程序被认为是这种表示的有力候选。

Method: 本文采用综述性研究方法，系统回顾了使用程序作为概念表示的计算模型，并从通用表示语言的角度对这些模型进行了评估。

Result: 通过对现有程序式概念学习模型的审查与评估，梳理了各模型在实现通用概念表示方面的进展与局限。

Conclusion: 程序因其结构化和可组合性，是概念通用表示的有力候选，但现有模型仍需进一步发展以实现真正的通用表示语言。

Abstract: Humans can learn and generalize novel concepts from sparse data because they express knowledge in rich structural formats. In this paper, we propose that programs are a strong candidate for universal representation of concepts. We review computational models of concept learning that use programs as their concept representation and evaluate their contribution toward a universal representational language.

</details>


### [162] [Data-Driven Discovery of Composition-Dependent Constitutive Models for Hyperelasticity and Viscoelasticity of Digital Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04541)
*Josué García-Ávila,Beijun Shen,Manuel K. Rausch,Mary C. Boyce,Adrián Buganza-Tepole*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的多材料本构建模框架，扩展了Bergström-Boyce模型，用于表征多材料3D打印数字材料的非线性、成分依赖及率依赖的粘弹性行为。该框架通过神经微分方程(NODEs)或闭环参数识别自动构建平衡分支的凸应变能函数，并通过受约束的人工神经网络学习非平衡分支动力学，在保持热力学一致性的同时捕获跨成分的率依赖刚度和滞回行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多材料3D打印的数字材料由刚性与柔性成分混合构成，表现出强非线性、成分依赖及率依赖的耗散行为。经典有限应变粘弹性模型依赖闭环应变能函数和内变量演化，当需要用单一本构模型推广到多种材料与加载速率时灵活性受限。

Method: 提出一个数据驱动的多材料本构建模框架，保留Bergström-Boyce模型的结构（乘法运动学、基于不变量的应变能函数、沿归一化非平衡偏应力方向的标量耗散演化律）。平衡分支要么直接预测闭环模型参数随成分的变化，要么利用神经微分方程(NODEs)自动构造多凸应变能函数；非平衡分支动力学通过直接识别闭环参数或使用适当约束的人工神经网络学习。

Result: 利用多种材料成分的多速率单轴压缩数据，验证了所提框架能够捕获跨成分的率依赖刚度和滞回行为，同时保持热力学一致性。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动框架在保留经典粘弹性模型结构的物理可解释性的同时，借助数据驱动发现方法实现了对多材料3D打印数字材料复杂行为的泛化建模，兼顾了热力学一致性与跨成分、跨速率的预测能力。

Abstract: Digital materials fabricated by multi-material 3D printing are designed as controlled mixtures of stiff and compliant constituents, yielding effective responses that span more than an order of magnitude in apparent stiffness and exhibit strongly nonlinear, composition-dependent, and rate-dependent dissipative behavior. Classical finite-strain viscoelastic models represent such behavior with closed-form strain energy functions for equilibrium and non-equilibrium stresses as well as evolution of internal variables, which may limit flexibility when a single constitutive model is expected to generalize across materials and loading rates. Here, we present a data-driven multi-material constitutive modeling framework that generalizes a formulation by Bergström and Boyce. The proposed framework retains the structure of the classical model, namely multiplicative kinematics, invariant-based strain-energy functions, and a scalar dissipative evolution law directed along the normalized nonequilibrium deviatoric stress. For the equilibrium branch, the data-driven discovery framework either directly predicts closed-form model parameters as functions of composition or automatically constructs a polyconvex strain-energy function using neural ordinary differential equations (NODEs). The nonequilibrium branch kinetics are learned similarly, either by directly identifying closed-form parameters across compositions or by using appropriately constrained artificial neural networks. Using multi-rate uniaxial compression data across multiple material compositions, we show that the proposed formulation captures rate-dependent stiffness and hysteresis across compositions while preserving thermodynamic consistency.

</details>


### [163] [From Answers to Interpretations: Rethinking Ambiguity-Induced Aleatoric Uncertainty Estimation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04543)
*Omer Nahum,Niv Nayman,Jonathan Fhima,Alon Zolfi,Jeremy Levy,Shai Mazor,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需生成答案即可直接从"解释空间"（interpretation space）估计由输入歧义引起的偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty）的方法，相比传统的"先生成澄清再让模型作答"的分解方法，该方法在歧义检测上提升了AUROC（63.34 vs 60.85），减少了4-26倍输出token和2.2-3.5倍API调用，且估计结果与认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）的相关性显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可靠的大语言模型部署中，需要区分不确定性源于任务本身固有的可变性（偶然不确定性）还是模型知识的局限（认知不确定性）。语言任务中偶然不确定性的核心来源是输入歧义或欠拟合（underspecification）。现有分解方法通过为输入生成多个澄清、让模型在每种澄清下作答并比较答案来估计偶然不确定性，但作者认为答案对于识别歧义并非必需：答案通常冗余、带来不必要的计算成本，并可能通过认知泄漏产生误导。

Method: 作者从理论上论证了答案为歧义识别所非必需，并提出了一种"仅澄清"（clarification-only）的方法：直接基于澄清所揭示的多种合理解释空间来估计由歧义引起的偶然不确定性成分，完全无需模型对澄清后的输入生成答案。实验以歧义检测作为运行层评估任务，在三个基准上进行验证。

Result: 该方法在三个基准上实现了更高的AUROC（63.34 vs 60.85），将输出token的计算开销降低4-26倍、API调用次数降低2.2-3.5倍，并且估计结果与认知不确定性的相关性大幅降低。

Conclusion: 结果表明，由歧义引起的偶然不确定性更适合从解释空间而非响应空间中估计，即通过澄清输入的空间直接刻画歧义，而不需要基于澄清后的回答进行比较。

Abstract: A key challenge in reliable LLM deployment is recognizing when uncertainty reflects irreducible variability in the task rather than limitations in the model's knowledge. In language tasks, a central source of such aleatoric uncertainty is input ambiguity or underspecification, where multiple interpretations remain plausible. Existing decomposition methods estimate aleatoric uncertainty by generating multiple clarifications of the input, querying the model for an answer under each clarification, and comparing the resulting answers. We argue that answers are not necessary for identifying ambiguity: they are often redundant, add avoidable cost, and can mislead through epistemic leakage. We support this claim theoretically, and propose a clarification-only approach that estimates this ambiguity-induced component directly from the space of plausible interpretations, without answers to the clarified inputs. Using ambiguity detection as an operational evaluation across three benchmarks, this direct approach improves AUROC (63.34 vs. 60.85), reduces computational cost by 4-26x in output tokens and 2.2-3.5x in API calls, and yields estimates with substantially lower correlation with epistemic uncertainty. Overall, our results suggest that ambiguity-induced aleatoric uncertainty is better estimated from the interpretation space than from the response space.

</details>


### [164] [IPGeoAI: Transformer-Based Geolocation with LLM Semantic Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04559)
*Avinash Kadimisetty,Andy Jinqing Yu,Philip Favaloro,Wenlong Liu,Xiaolu Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IPGeoAI是一种将城市级IP地理定位从静态查找重构为序列建模任务的深度学习模型，利用Transformer Encoder捕捉IP子网层级依赖，并通过零样本LLM特征提取将AS描述转换为结构化语义元数据，经多头交叉注意力融合进网络。离线评估在20万城市数据上超越领先外部厂商6%的城市级准确率并达到100%覆盖率，在线生产测试中带来+0.35%的下游业务指标显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统启发式方法和数据库驱动方法难以解析现代网络基础设施中复杂、非线性的地址分配模式，尤其在IPv6地址空间快速增长和移动网络短暂性的背景下，城市级IP地理定位的准确性面临瓶颈。

Method: 提出IPGeoAI架构：1) 采用Transformer Encoder将地理定位从静态查找重构为序列建模，捕捉IP子网结构中的层级依赖；2) 通过Zero-Shot LLM Feature Extraction pipeline，利用大语言模型将原始且嘈杂的AS描述离线预计算为结构化领域元数据（如University vs ISP、Global vs Local）；3) 通过Multi-Head Cross-Attention模块将语义信号融合进网络，桥接数值网络拓扑与真实世界语义身份之间的鸿沟；4) 采用分层推理策略，从粗粒度国家信号精化到城市级定位。

Result: 在覆盖20万城市的专有数据集上，IPGeoAI在城市级粒度上显著超越领先的外部厂商；通过分层推理策略精化国家信号，实现城市级准确率6%的提升，并将覆盖率扩展到100%的流量。大规模在线生产测试中，模型为第一梯队下游用例指标带来了统计学显著的+0.35%提升。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM语义特征与Transformer序列建模相结合，IPGeoAI有效弥合了数值网络拓扑与现实世界语义身份之间的鸿沟，验证了从静态查表转向序列建模范式在IP地理定位任务中的有效性与实用性，并在大规模实际部署中展现出稳定的业务价值。

Abstract: Accurate city-level IP Geolocation is an important enabler for the modern digital ecosystem, underpinning services ranging from local content delivery and targeting to digital rights enforcement. However, traditional heuristic and database-driven methods often struggle to resolve the complex, non-linear allocation patterns of modern network infrastructures, particularly within the exploding IPv6 address space and transient mobile networks. In this paper, we introduce IPGeoAI, a novel deep learning model architecture that reframes geolocation from a static lookup problem to a sequential modeling task. Our approach utilizes the Transformer Encoder to capture hierarchical dependencies inherent in IP subnet structures. We propose a method to resolve geographic ambiguity by integrating unstructured semantic context via a Zero-Shot LLM Feature Extraction pipeline. We utilize Large Language Models to transform raw, noisy Autonomous Systems (AS) descriptions into structured, domain-specific metadata (such as 'University' vs. 'ISP' or 'Global' vs. 'Local') via an offline pre-computation process. By fusing these semantic signals into the network via a Multi-Head Cross-Attention module, we bridge the gap between numerical network topology and real-world semantic identity. Extensive offline evaluation on a proprietary dataset spanning 200,000 cities demonstrates that IPGeoAI significantly outperforms a leading external vendor in city-level granularity. By adopting a hierarchical inference strategy that refines coarse-grained country signals, our model achieves a 6% improvement in city-level accuracy while extending coverage to 100% of the traffic. Furthermore, in large-scale online production tests, the model drove a statistically significant +0.35% improvement in our 1st-tier downstream use cases metric.

</details>


### [165] [Reducing Hallucinated Transcripts in Whisper via Hallucination Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04561)
*Maryam Abbasihafshejani,Murtuza Jadliwala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的推理时方法，利用低秩投影将Whisper解码器激活从幻觉相关子空间中投影出去，从而在不重新训练模型的情况下大幅抑制ASR模型的幻觉转录。在非语音基准上，始终开启的投影将幻觉率从31.31%降至2.44%（相对降低92.21%），门控投影则降至3.74%（相对降低88.05%），同时在语音识别性能上提供可控的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
